import type { Env, ReviewReport, ReviewRunResponse } from '../../types.js';
import {
  appendReviewEvent,
  getHighestFindingNumberForBranch,
  replaceReviewFindings,
  updateReviewRunStatus,
} from '../db.js';

function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) ? (value as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
}

function readOptionalString(value: unknown): string | null {
  return typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() ? value.trim() : null;
}

/**
 * Persists successful review output, assigns branch-stable finding numbers, and emits completion events.
 */
export async function finalizeSuccessfulReview(
  env: Env,
  reviewId: string,
  payload: Record<string, unknown>,
  report: ReviewReport
): Promise<void> {
  const payloadRecord = asRecord(payload);
  const requestProvenance = asRecord(payloadRecord.provenance);
  const reviewRepo = readOptionalString(requestProvenance.repo);
  const reviewBranch = readOptionalString(requestProvenance.branch);
  if (!reviewRepo || !reviewBranch) {
    throw new Error('Review request payload missing required provenance.repo or provenance.branch.');
  }

  const findingSequenceStart = (await getHighestFindingNumberForBranch(env.DB, reviewRepo, reviewBranch)) + 1;
  const findingsWithSequence = report.findings.map((finding, index) => ({
    ...finding,
    sequence: findingSequenceStart + index,
  }));
  const reportWithSequence: ReviewReport = {
    ...report,
    findings: findingsWithSequence,
  };

  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
    reviewId,
    eventType: 'review_finalize_started',
    payload: {
      findingCount: report.findings.length,
    },
  });
  await replaceReviewFindings(env.DB, reviewId, findingsWithSequence, { startNumber: findingSequenceStart });
  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
    reviewId,
    eventType: 'review_analysis_findings_persisted',
    payload: {
      findingCount: report.findings.length,
    },
  });
  await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'succeeded', {
    report: reportWithSequence,
    markdownSummary: reportWithSequence.markdownSummary,
    errorCode: null,
    errorMessage: null,
  });
  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
    reviewId,
    eventType: 'review_analysis_succeeded',
    payload: {
      findingCount: report.findings.length,
    },
  });
  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
    reviewId,
    eventType: 'review_succeeded',
    payload: {
      recommendation: report.summary.recommendation,
      findingCount: report.findings.length,
    },
  });
}

/**
 * Persists a terminal failed state and emits failure events, including context-assembly failures when applicable.
 */
export async function finalizeFailedReview(
  env: Env,
  reviewId: string,
  input: {
    errorCode: string;
    message: string;
    contextAssemblyErrorCode?: string | null;
  }
): Promise<void> {
  await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'failed', {
    errorCode: input.errorCode,
    errorMessage: input.message,
  });
  try {
    if (input.contextAssemblyErrorCode) {
      await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
        reviewId,
        eventType: 'review_context_assembly_failed',
        payload: {
          code: input.contextAssemblyErrorCode,
          message: input.message,
        },
      });
    }
    await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
      reviewId,
      eventType: 'review_failed',
      payload: {
        code: input.errorCode,
        message: input.message,
      },
    });
  } catch {
    // Best-effort terminal event.
  }
}

diff --git a/packages/cli/test/app/reviews/studio-preflight-cache.test.ts b/packages/cli/test/app/reviews/studio-preflight-cache.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8bb84a5

@@ -0,0 +1,80 @@
+import { strict as assert } from 'assert';
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'os';
+import { join } from 'path';
+import {
+  getStudioNewReviewPreflightCached,
+  resetStudioPreflightCacheForTests,
+  setStudioPreflightResolverForTests,
+} from '../../../src/app/reviews/studio-preflight-cache.js';
+import type { StudioNewReviewPreflightResult } from '../../../src/app/reviews/studio-create.js';
+
+function createPreflightResult(lastCheckpoints: 1 | 2 | 3): StudioNewReviewPreflightResult {
+  return {
+    repo: 'dayhaysoos/nimbus',
+    branch: 'feature/test',
+    policyMode: 'auto',
+    lastCheckpoints,
+    checkpointSelectionMode: lastCheckpoints > 1 ? 'last_n' : 'latest',
+    checkpointId: `${lastCheckpoints}`.repeat(12),
+    commitSha: `${lastCheckpoints}`.repeat(40),
+    includedCheckpoints: [],
+    ready: true,
+    checks: [
+      {
+        code: 'checkpoint',
+        label: 'Checkpoint target',
+        ok: true,
+        detail: `Resolved selection ${lastCheckpoints}.`,
+      },
+      {
+        code: 'entire_context',
+        label: 'Entire context',
+        ok: true,
+        detail: 'Readable Entire checkpoint context found for current commit.',
+      },
+    ],
+  };
+}
+
+export async function runStudioPreflightCacheTests(): Promise<void> {
+  const repoRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'nimbus-studio-preflight-cache-'));
+  try {
+    resetStudioPreflightCacheForTests();
+    const pendingByWindow = new Map<1 | 2 | 3, Promise<StudioNewReviewPreflightResult>>();
+    const resolveByWindow = new Map<1 | 2 | 3, (value: StudioNewReviewPreflightResult) => void>();
+    const calls: Array<1 | 2 | 3> = [];
+
+    setStudioPreflightResolverForTests(async (options) => {
+      const normalized = (options?.lastCheckpoints ?? 2) as 1 | 2 | 3;
+      calls.push(normalized);
+      const existing = pendingByWindow.get(normalized);
+      if (existing) {
+        return existing;
+      }
+      const pending = new Promise<StudioNewReviewPreflightResult>((resolve) => {
+        resolveByWindow.set(normalized, resolve);
+      });
+      pendingByWindow.set(normalized, pending);
+      return pending;
+    });
+
+    const twoPromise = getStudioNewReviewPreflightCached({ repoRoot, lastCheckpoints: 2 });
+    const onePromise = getStudioNewReviewPreflightCached({ repoRoot, lastCheckpoints: 1 });
+
+    assert.deepEqual(calls, [2, 1]);
+
+    resolveByWindow.get(2)?.(createPreflightResult(2));
+    resolveByWindow.get(1)?.(createPreflightResult(1));
+
+    const [twoResult, oneResult] = await Promise.all([twoPromise, onePromise]);
+    assert.equal(twoResult.lastCheckpoints, 2);
+    assert.equal(oneResult.lastCheckpoints, 1);
+    assert.equal(twoResult.checkpointId, '222222222222');
+    assert.equal(oneResult.checkpointId, '111111111111');
+  } finally {
+    setStudioPreflightResolverForTests(null);
+    resetStudioPreflightCacheForTests();
+    await rm(repoRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+}
diff --git a/packages/cli/test/commands/review/preflight-selection.test.ts b/packages/cli/test/commands/review/preflight-selection.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ca9640c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/cli/test/commands/review/preflight-selection.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+import { strict as assert } from 'assert';
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile } from 'fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'os';
+import { join } from 'path';
+import { execFileSync } from 'child_process';
+import { validateReviewCommitCheckpoint } from '../../../src/commands/review/preflight.js';
+
+function git(cwd: string, args: string[]): string {
+  return execFileSync('git', args, {
+    cwd,
+    encoding: 'utf8',
+    stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
+  }).trim();
+}
+
+async function writeAndCommit(
+  repoDir: string,
+  input: {
+    filePath: string;
+    content: string;
+    message: string;
+  }
+): Promise<string> {
+  await writeFile(join(repoDir, input.filePath), input.content, 'utf8');
+  git(repoDir, ['add', input.filePath]);
+  git(repoDir, ['commit', '-m', input.message]);
+  return git(repoDir, ['rev-parse', 'HEAD']);
+}
+
+export async function runReviewPreflightSelectionTests(): Promise<void> {
+  const repoDir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'nimbus-review-preflight-'));
+  try {
+    git(repoDir, ['init', '-b', 'main']);
+    git(repoDir, ['config', 'user.name', 'Nimbus Tests']);
+    git(repoDir, ['config', 'user.email', 'nimbus-tests@example.com']);
+
+    await writeAndCommit(repoDir, {
+      filePath: 'app.txt',
+      content: 'one\n',
+      message: 'feat: checkpoint one\n\nEntire-Checkpoint: aaaaaaaaaaaa',
+    });
+    const secondCheckpointSha = await writeAndCommit(repoDir, {
+      filePath: 'app.txt',
+      content: 'one\ntwo\n',
+      message: 'feat: checkpoint two\n\nEntire-Checkpoint: bbbbbbbbbbbb',
+    });
+    const nonCheckpointHeadSha = await writeAndCommit(repoDir, {
+      filePath: 'app.txt',
+      content: 'one\ntwo\nthree\n',
+      message: 'feat: plain head commit',
+    });
+
+    assert.throws(
+      () => validateReviewCommitCheckpoint('HEAD', repoDir, { lastCheckpoints: 2 }),
+      /This commit has no Entire-Checkpoint trailer\. The last commit on this branch with valid checkpoint context was/
+    );
+
+    const checkpointedHeadSha = await writeAndCommit(repoDir, {
+      filePath: 'app.txt',
+      content: 'one\ntwo\nthree\nfour\n',
+      message: 'feat: checkpoint three\n\nEntire-Checkpoint: cccccccccccc',
+    });
+
+    const resolved = validateReviewCommitCheckpoint('HEAD', repoDir, { lastCheckpoints: 2 });
+    assert.equal(resolved.commitSha, checkpointedHeadSha);
+    assert.equal(resolved.checkpointId, 'cccccccccccc');
+    assert.equal(resolved.checkpointSelectionMode, 'last_n');
+    assert.deepEqual(
+      resolved.includedCheckpoints?.map((checkpoint) => checkpoint.commitSha),
+      [secondCheckpointSha, checkpointedHeadSha]
+    );
+    assert.equal(resolved.commitDiffPatch.includes('three'), true);
+    assert.equal(resolved.commitDiffPatch.includes('four'), true);
+    assert.equal(resolved.commitDiffPatch.includes(nonCheckpointHeadSha.slice(0, 7)), false);
+  } finally {
+    await rm(repoDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+}

+  assert.deepEqual(computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(8), {
+    deterministicMaxSteps: 2,
+    providerMaxSteps: 6,
+  });
+  assert.deepEqual(computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(18), {
+    deterministicMaxSteps: 12,
+    providerMaxSteps: 6,
+  });
+
+  const queries = extractDeterministicSearchQueriesForTests([
+    {
+      path: 'packages/worker/src/api/reviews/recovery.ts',
+      content: `
+import { updateReviewRunStatus, replaceReviewFindings } from '../../lib/db.js';
+import { createReviewQueueMessage } from '../../lib/review-queue.js';
+
+export async function manuallyRecoverReviewRun() {
+  await updateReviewRunStatus();
+  await replaceReviewFindings();
+  return createReviewQueueMessage();
+}
+      `,
+      byteSize: 240,
+      source: 'changed',
+    },
+  ]);
+
+  assert.deepEqual(
+    queries,
+    ['updateReviewRunStatus', 'replaceReviewFindings', 'createReviewQueueMessage']
+  );
+
+  const integrationQueries = extractIntegrationSearchQueriesForTests([

+      path: 'packages/report-ui/src/components/ReportPage.tsx',
+      content: `
+const response = await fetch(\`${'${API_BASE}'}/api/reviews/\${encodeURIComponent(reviewId)}/recover\`, {
+  method: 'POST',
+});
+if (review.status === 'running') {
+  return null;
+}
+      `,
+      byteSize: 240,
+      source: 'changed',
+    },
+    {
+      path: 'packages/cli/src/app/reviews/ui-proxy.ts',
+      content: `
+import { startStudioNewReview } from './studio-create.js';
+
+await startStudioNewReview({
+  onEvent: async (event) => {
+    return event;
+  },
+});
+      `,

+      source: 'changed',
+    },
+    {
+      path: 'packages/cli/src/app/reviews/context.ts',
+      content: `
+export async function emitResolveReviewProgress(options, event) {

   125	
   126	function inferFollowUpReviewScore(output: { findings: ReviewFinding[]; furtherPassesLowYield: boolean }): 1 | 2 | 3 {
   127	  const severities = new Set(output.findings.map((finding) => finding.severity));
   128	  if (severities.has('critical') || severities.has('high')) {
   129	    return 3;
   130	  }
   131	  if (severities.has('medium')) {
   132	    return 2;
   133	  }
   134	  if (output.findings.length === 0) {
   135	    return output.furtherPassesLowYield ? 1 : 2;
   136	  }
   137	  return 1;
   138	}
   139	
   140	function deriveFollowUpReviewMetadata(
   141	  payload: unknown,
   142	  output: { findings: ReviewFinding[]; furtherPassesLowYield: boolean }
   143	): { score: 1 | 2 | 3; rationale: string } {
   144	  const record = asRecord(payload);
   145	  const rawScore = typeof record.followUpReviewScore === 'number' ? Math.floor(record.followUpReviewScore) : null;
   146	  const score = rawScore === 1 || rawScore === 2 || rawScore === 3 ? rawScore : inferFollowUpReviewScore(output);
   147	  const rationaleCandidate = typeof record.followUpReviewRationale === 'string' ? record.followUpReviewRationale.trim() : '';
   148	  const rationale =
   149	    rationaleCandidate ||
   150	    (score === 3
   151	      ? 'High-severity findings remain in changed paths; another review pass is required after fixes.'
   152	      : score === 2
   153	        ? 'At least one non-trivial issue remains; a follow-up review pass is recommended after fixes.'
   154	        : 'Findings are low severity or low signal; additional review passes are likely diminishing returns.');
   155	
   156	  return { score, rationale };
   157	}

   170	function parseCompleteActionPayload(action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'complete' }>): unknown {
   171	  if (action.finalOutput !== undefined && action.finalOutput !== null) {
   172	    return action.finalOutput;
   173	  }
   174	  if (typeof action.summary === 'string' && action.summary.trim()) {
   175	    return parseJsonOutput(action.summary);
   176	  }
   177	  throw new ReviewAgentOutputError(
   178	    'Review agent complete action requires finalOutput or a summary containing valid JSON payload'
   179	  ).withCode(
   180	    'review_analysis_invalid_output',
   181	    {
   182	      errors: [
   183	        {
   184	          path: '$',
   185	          message: 'Complete action must include finalOutput or summary containing valid JSON payload.',
   186	        },
   187	      ],
   188	    }
   189	  );

   206	function parseReviewReasoningEffort(value: string | null | undefined): ReviewReasoningEffort | undefined {
   207	  if (typeof value !== 'string') {
   208	    return undefined;
   209	  }
   210	  const normalized = value.trim().toLowerCase();
   211	  return normalized === 'minimal' || normalized === 'low' || normalized === 'medium' || normalized === 'high'
   212	    ? normalized
   213	    : undefined;
   214	}

   216	function normalizePath(value: unknown): string | null {
   217	  if (typeof value !== 'string') {
   218	    return null;
   219	  }
   220	  const trimmed = value.trim().replace(/\\/g, '/');
   221	  return trimmed ? trimmed : null;
   222	}
   223	
   224	function recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(
   225	  state: ReviewEvidenceState,
   226	  action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }>,
   227	  toolOutput: unknown,
   228	  changedPaths: Set<string>
   229	): void {
   230	  if (action.tool === 'diff_summary') {
   231	    state.diffSummaryUsed = true;
   232	    return;
   233	  }
   234	
   235	  if (action.tool === 'read_file') {
   236	    const readPath = normalizePath(action.args.path);
   237	    if (readPath) {
   238	      if (changedPaths.has(readPath)) {
   239	        state.readChangedPaths.add(readPath);
   240	      } else {
   241	        state.readCrossFilePaths.add(readPath);
   242	      }
   243	    }
   244	    return;
   245	  }
   246	
   247	  if (action.tool === 'read_batch') {
   248	    const files = Array.isArray(asRecord(toolOutput).files) ? (asRecord(toolOutput).files as unknown[]) : [];
   249	    for (const file of files) {
   250	      const readPath = normalizePath(asRecord(file).path);
   251	      if (!readPath) {
   252	        continue;
   253	      }
   254	      if (changedPaths.has(readPath)) {
   255	        state.readChangedPaths.add(readPath);
   256	      } else {
   257	        state.readCrossFilePaths.add(readPath);
   258	      }
   259	    }
   260	    return;
   261	  }
   262	
   263	  if (action.tool === 'search_code') {
   264	    state.searchUsed = true;
   265	    const matches = Array.isArray(asRecord(toolOutput).matches) ? (asRecord(toolOutput).matches as unknown[]) : [];
   266	    state.searchMatchedChangedPath = matches.some((match) => {
   267	      const matchPath = normalizePath(asRecord(match).path);
   268	      return Boolean(matchPath && changedPaths.has(matchPath));
   269	    });
   270	  }
   271	}
   272	
   273	function collectMissingEvidenceRequirements(input: {
   274	  evidence: ReviewEvidenceState;
   275	  changedPaths: string[];
   276	  requiresCrossFileEvidence: boolean;
   277	}): string[] {
   278	  const missing: string[] = [];
   279	  const sensitiveChangedPaths = input.changedPaths.filter((path) => isSensitiveChangedPath(path));
   280	
   281	  if (input.changedPaths.length > 0 && !input.evidence.diffSummaryUsed) {
   282	    missing.push('Run diff_summary at least once before completing analysis.');
   283	  }
   284	
   285	  const requiredReadCoverage =
   286	    input.changedPaths.length <= MAX_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE_REQUIREMENT
   287	      ? input.changedPaths.length
   288	      : Math.min(MIN_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE, input.changedPaths.length);
   289	  if (input.evidence.readChangedPaths.size < requiredReadCoverage) {
   290	    missing.push(
   291	      `Read at least ${requiredReadCoverage} changed file(s) directly with read_file (currently ${input.evidence.readChangedPaths.size}).`
   292	    );
   293	  }
   294	
   295	  const missingSensitivePaths = sensitiveChangedPaths.filter((path) => !input.evidence.readChangedPaths.has(path));
   296	  if (missingSensitivePaths.length > 0) {
   297	    missing.push(`Read sensitive changed file(s) directly: ${missingSensitivePaths.join(', ')}.`);
   298	  }
   299	
   300	  if (input.requiresCrossFileEvidence && input.evidence.readCrossFilePaths.size === 0) {
   301	    missing.push('Read at least one non-changed file that defines or handles an integration boundary touched by the diff.');
   302	  }
   303	
   304	  if (input.changedPaths.length > 0 && !input.evidence.searchUsed) {
   305	    missing.push('Run search_code at least once before completing analysis.');
   306	  }
   307	
   308	  return missing;
   309	}
   310	
   311	function selectDeterministicReadPaths(changedPaths: string[]): string[] {
   312	  const deduped = Array.from(new Set(changedPaths));
   313	  const sensitive = deduped.filter((path) => isSensitiveChangedPath(path));
   314	  const requiredCount =
   315	    deduped.length <= MAX_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE_REQUIREMENT
   316	      ? deduped.length
   317	      : Math.min(MIN_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE, deduped.length);
   318	  const selected = [...sensitive];
   319	  for (const path of deduped) {
   320	    if (selected.length >= Math.max(requiredCount, sensitive.length)) {
   321	      break;
   322	    }
   323	    if (!selected.includes(path)) {
   324	      selected.push(path);
   325	    }
   326	  }
   327	  return selected.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_READ_PATHS);
   328	}
   329	
   330	function pathStem(path: string): string | null {
   331	  const fileName = path.split('/').pop() ?? '';
   332	  const stem = fileName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '').trim();
   333	  if (stem.length < DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_MIN_QUERY_LENGTH) {
   334	    return null;
   335	  }
   336	  return stem;
   337	}
   338	
   339	function maybeAddSearchQuery(queries: string[], seen: Set<string>, candidate: string): void {
   340	  const normalized = candidate.trim();
   341	  if (normalized.length < DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_MIN_QUERY_LENGTH) {
   342	    return;
   343	  }
   344	  if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(normalized)) {
   345	    return;
   346	  }
   347	  const lowered = normalized.toLowerCase();
   348	  if (IGNORABLE_SEARCH_TOKENS.has(lowered)) {
   349	    return;
   350	  }
   351	  if (seen.has(lowered)) {
   352	    return;
   353	  }
   354	  seen.add(lowered);
   355	  queries.push(normalized);
   356	}
   357	
   358	type DeterministicSearchKind = 'api_route' | 'imported_symbol' | 'callback' | 'dependency';
   359	
   360	interface DeterministicSearchQuery {
   361	  query: string;
   362	  sourcePath: string;
   363	  kind: DeterministicSearchKind;
   364	}
   365	
   366	function maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(
   367	  queries: DeterministicSearchQuery[],
   368	  seen: Set<string>,
   369	  candidate: string,
   370	  sourcePath: string,
   371	  kind: DeterministicSearchKind
   372	): void {
   373	  const tempQueries: string[] = [];
   374	  const beforeSize = seen.size;
   375	  maybeAddSearchQuery(tempQueries, seen, candidate);
   376	  if (seen.size > beforeSize && tempQueries.length > 0) {
   377	    queries.push({ query: tempQueries[0], sourcePath, kind });
   378	  }
   379	}
   380	
   381	function extractDeterministicSearchQueries(changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']): DeterministicSearchQuery[] {
   382	  const queries: DeterministicSearchQuery[] = [];
   383	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   384	  const sensitiveFiles = changedFiles.filter((file) => isSensitiveChangedPath(file.path));
   385	  for (const file of sensitiveFiles) {
   386	    const importMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\bimport\s+(?:type\s+)?(?:\{([^}]+)\}|([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*))/g);
   387	    for (const match of importMatches) {
   388	      const importedBlock = match[1] ?? match[2] ?? '';
   389	      for (const item of importedBlock.split(',')) {
   390	        const cleaned = item.replace(/\bas\b.+$/i, '').trim();
   391	        maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, cleaned, file.path, 'dependency');
   392	        if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   393	          return queries;
   394	        }
   395	      }
   396	    }
   397	
   398	    const callMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\b([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{3,})\s*\(/g);
   399	    for (const match of callMatches) {
   400	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, match[1] ?? '', file.path, 'dependency');
   401	      if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   402	        return queries;
   403	      }
   404	    }
   405	
   406	    const stem = pathStem(file.path);
   407	    if (stem) {
   408	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, stem, file.path, 'dependency');
   409	      if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   410	        return queries;
   411	      }
   412	    }
   413	  }
   414	
   415	  if (queries.length === 0) {
   416	    const fallbackStem = changedFiles.map((file) => pathStem(file.path)).find(Boolean);
   417	    if (fallbackStem) {
   418	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, fallbackStem, changedFiles[0]?.path ?? '.', 'dependency');
   419	    }
   420	  }
   421	
   422	  return queries.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES);
   423	}
   424	
   425	export function extractDeterministicSearchQueriesForTests(
   426	  changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']
   427	): string[] {
   428	  return extractDeterministicSearchQueries(changedFiles).map((entry) => entry.query);
   429	}
   430	
   431	function extractStaticApiTailQueries(content: string): string[] {
   432	  const queries: string[] = [];
   433	  const lines = content.split('\n');
   434	  for (const line of lines) {
   435	    if (!line.includes('/api/')) {
   436	      continue;
   437	    }
   438	    const segments = Array.from(line.matchAll(/\/([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{3,})(?=(?:\/|\$|['"`)}\],; ]))/g))
   439	      .map((match) => match[1] ?? '')
   440	      .filter(Boolean);
   441	    for (let index = segments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
   442	      const segment = segments[index];
   443	      if (segment === 'api' || segment === 'review' || segment === 'reviews' || segment === 'workspace' || segment === 'workspaces') {
   444	        continue;
   445	      }
   446	      queries.push(`/${segment}`);
   447	      break;
   448	    }
   449	  }
   450	  return queries;
   451	}
   452	
   453	function extractIntegrationSearchQueries(changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']): DeterministicSearchQuery[] {
   454	  const queries: DeterministicSearchQuery[] = [];
   455	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   456	
   457	  for (const file of changedFiles) {
   458	    for (const query of extractStaticApiTailQueries(file.content)) {
   459	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, query, file.path, 'api_route');
   460	      if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   461	        return queries;
   462	      }
   463	    }
   464	
   465	    const importedNames = new Set<string>();
   466	    const importMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\bimport\s+(?:type\s+)?(?:\{([^}]+)\}|([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*))/g);
   467	    for (const match of importMatches) {
   468	      const importedBlock = match[1] ?? match[2] ?? '';
   469	      for (const item of importedBlock.split(',')) {
   470	        const cleaned = item.replace(/\bas\b.+$/i, '').trim();
   471	        if (!cleaned || !INTEGRATION_SYMBOL_KEYWORDS.test(cleaned)) {
   472	          continue;
   473	        }
   474	        importedNames.add(cleaned);
   475	      }
   476	    }
   477	    let importedQueriesAdded = 0;
   478	    for (const importedName of importedNames) {
   479	      if (!new RegExp(`\\b${importedName}\\s*\\(`).test(file.content)) {
   480	        continue;
   481	      }
   482	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, importedName, file.path, 'imported_symbol');
   483	      importedQueriesAdded += 1;
   484	      if (
   485	        queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES ||
   486	        importedQueriesAdded >= MAX_INTEGRATION_IMPORTED_SYMBOL_QUERIES_PER_FILE
   487	      ) {
   488	        break;
   489	      }
   490	    }
   491	    if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   492	      return queries;
   493	    }
   494	
   495	    let callbackQueriesAdded = 0;
   496	    const callbackMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\bon[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9_]{3,}\b/g);
   497	    for (const match of callbackMatches) {
   498	      const callbackName = match[0] ?? '';
   499	      if (!INTEGRATION_CALLBACK_PATTERN.test(callbackName)) {
   500	        continue;
   501	      }
   502	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, callbackName, file.path, 'callback');
   503	      callbackQueriesAdded += 1;
   504	      if (
   505	        queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES ||
   506	        callbackQueriesAdded >= MAX_INTEGRATION_CALLBACK_QUERIES_PER_FILE
   507	      ) {
   508	        break;
   509	      }
   510	    }
   511	    if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   512	      return queries;
   513	    }
   514	  }
   515	
   516	  return queries.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES);
   517	}

   520	  changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']

   561	  return score;

   563	
   564	function combineDeterministicSearchQueries(
   565	  primary: DeterministicSearchQuery[],
   566	  secondary: DeterministicSearchQuery[]
   567	): DeterministicSearchQuery[] {
   568	  const combined: DeterministicSearchQuery[] = [];
   569	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   570	  for (const query of [...primary, ...secondary]) {
   571	    const lowered = query.query.trim().toLowerCase();
   572	    if (!lowered || seen.has(lowered)) {
   573	      continue;
   574	    }
   575	    seen.add(lowered);
   576	    combined.push(query);
   577	    if (combined.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   578	      break;
   579	    }
   580	  }
   581	  return combined;
   582	}
   583	
   584	function pathDistanceScore(sourcePath: string, candidatePath: string): number {
   585	  const sourceSegments = sourcePath.split('/');
   586	  const candidateSegments = candidatePath.split('/');
   587	  let shared = 0;
   588	  while (
   589	    shared < sourceSegments.length &&
   590	    shared < candidateSegments.length &&
   591	    sourceSegments[shared] === candidateSegments[shared]
   592	  ) {
   593	    shared += 1;
   594	  }
   595	  return (sourceSegments.length - shared) + (candidateSegments.length - shared);
   596	}

   598	export function computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(maxSteps: number): { deterministicMaxSteps: number; providerMaxSteps: number } {
   599	  const providerMaxSteps = Math.max(1, maxSteps - Math.max(0, maxSteps - MIN_PROVIDER_REASONING_STEPS));
   600	  const deterministicMaxSteps = Math.max(0, maxSteps - providerMaxSteps);
   601	  return { deterministicMaxSteps, providerMaxSteps };
   602	}

   604	async function runDeterministicEvidenceCollection(input: {
   605	  sandbox: SandboxClient;
   606	  policy: ReviewCommandPolicy;
   607	  maxFileBytes: number;
   608	  authoritativeDiffSnapshot: unknown;
   609	  changedPaths: string[];
   610	  changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles'];
   611	  changedPathsSet: Set<string>;
   612	  evidence: ReviewEvidenceState;
   613	  usedTools: string[];
   614	  history: ReviewAgentHistoryEntry[];
   615	  maxDeterministicSteps: number;
   616	  onLifecycleEvent?: (eventType: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) => void | Promise<void>;
   617	}): Promise<number> {
   618	  const deterministicTools: Array<Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }>> = [
   619	    { type: 'tool', tool: 'diff_summary', args: { maxBytes: 64_000 } },
   620	    ...selectDeterministicReadPaths(input.changedPaths).map((path) => ({
   621	      type: 'tool' as const,
   622	      tool: 'read_file' as const,
   623	      args: { path, maxBytes: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 48_000) },
   624	    })),
   625	  ];
   626	  const followUpReadPaths: string[] = [];
   627	
   628	  let deterministicStep = 0;
   629	  for (const action of deterministicTools) {
   630	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   631	      break;
   632	    }
   633	    deterministicStep += 1;
   634	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   635	      input.sandbox,
   636	      action,
   637	      input.policy,
   638	      input.maxFileBytes,
   639	      action.tool === 'diff_summary' ? input.authoritativeDiffSnapshot : undefined
   640	    );
   641	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   642	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   643	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   644	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   645	        step: deterministicStep,
   646	        tool: action.tool,
   647	        deterministic: true,
   648	      });
   649	    }
   650	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   651	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   652	  }
   653	
   654	  const deterministicSearchQueries = combineDeterministicSearchQueries(
   655	    extractIntegrationSearchQueries(input.changedFiles),
   656	    extractDeterministicSearchQueries(input.changedFiles)
   657	  );
   658	  for (const searchQuery of deterministicSearchQueries) {
   659	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   660	      break;
   661	    }
   662	    const action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }> = {
   663	      type: 'tool',
   664	      tool: 'search_code',
   665	      args: {
   666	        query: searchQuery.query,
   667	        path: '.',
   668	        maxResults: 20,
   669	        maxBytesPerFile: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 16_000),
   670	      },
   671	    };
   672	    deterministicStep += 1;
   673	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   674	      input.sandbox,
   675	      action,
   676	      input.policy,
   677	      input.maxFileBytes,
   678	    );
   679	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   680	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   681	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   682	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   683	        step: deterministicStep,
   684	        tool: action.tool,
   685	        deterministic: true,
   686	        query: searchQuery.query,
   687	      });
   688	    }
   689	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   690	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   691	
   692	    const matches = Array.isArray(asRecord(output.result).matches) ? (asRecord(output.result).matches as unknown[]) : [];
   693	    const rankedMatches = matches
   694	      .map((match) => {
   695	        const matchPath = normalizePath(asRecord(match).path);
   696	        return matchPath
   697	          ? {
   698	              matchPath,
   699	              score: scoreSearchMatch({
   700	                sourcePath: searchQuery.sourcePath,
   701	                candidatePath: matchPath,
   702	                kind: searchQuery.kind,
   703	                query: searchQuery.query,
   704	              }),
   705	            }
   706	          : null;
   707	      })
   708	      .filter((match): match is { matchPath: string; score: number } => Boolean(match))
   709	      .sort((left, right) => left.score - right.score);
   710	    for (const match of rankedMatches) {
   711	      const matchPath = match.matchPath;
   712	      if (input.changedPathsSet.has(matchPath) || followUpReadPaths.includes(matchPath)) {
   713	        continue;
   714	      }
   715	      followUpReadPaths.push(matchPath);
   716	      if (followUpReadPaths.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_CROSS_FILE_READS) {
   717	        break;
   718	      }
   719	    }
   720	  }
   721	
   722	  for (const path of followUpReadPaths) {
   723	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   724	      break;
   725	    }
   726	    const batchPaths = [path, ...followUpReadPaths.filter((candidate) => candidate !== path)].slice(0, 2);
   727	    const action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }> = {
   728	      type: 'tool',
   729	      tool: 'read_batch',
   730	      args: { paths: batchPaths, maxBytes: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 32_000) },
   731	    };
   732	    deterministicStep += 1;
   733	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   734	      input.sandbox,
   735	      action,
   736	      input.policy,
   737	      input.maxFileBytes,
   738	    );
   739	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   740	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   741	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   742	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   743	        step: deterministicStep,
   744	        tool: action.tool,
   745	        deterministic: true,
   746	        crossFile: true,
   747	      });
   748	    }
   749	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   750	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   751	    break;
   752	  }
   753	
   754	  return deterministicStep;
   755	}

   757	/**
   758	 * Hydrates a temporary sandbox from a stored source bundle and reads a bounded set of files
   759	 * through the same read-only tool path used by review analysis.
   760	 */
   761	export async function readWorkspaceFilesFromSourceBundle(
   762	  env: Env,
   763	  input: {
   764	    sourceBundleKey: string;
   765	    sandboxId: string;
   766	    paths: string[];
   767	    maxFileBytes?: number;
   768	  }
   769	): Promise<ReviewSourceFileReadResult[]> {
   770	  const maxFileBytes = parseIntegerString(env.REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_FILE_BYTES, DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_FILE_BYTES, 1_024, 200_000);
   771	  const effectiveMaxFileBytes =
   772	    typeof input.maxFileBytes === 'number' && Number.isFinite(input.maxFileBytes)
   773	      ? Math.max(1_024, Math.min(maxFileBytes, Math.floor(input.maxFileBytes)))
   774	      : maxFileBytes;
   775	
   776	  if (!env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS && !env.SOURCE_BUNDLES) {
   777	    throw new Error('WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS or SOURCE_BUNDLES binding is required for review context assembly');
   778	  }
   779	  const bundle =
   780	    (env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS ? await env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null) ??
   781	    (env.SOURCE_BUNDLES ? await env.SOURCE_BUNDLES.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null);
   782	  if (!bundle) {
   783	    throw new Error(`Review source bundle not found: ${input.sourceBundleKey}`);
   784	  }
   785	
   786	  const sandbox = await resolveReviewSandbox(env, input.sandboxId);
   787	  try {
   788	    await hydrateReviewSandbox(sandbox, await bundle.arrayBuffer());
   789	
   790	    const policy: ReviewCommandPolicy = {
   791	      commandAllow: [],
   792	      commandDeny: [],
   793	      maxCommandTimeoutMs: MAX_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_MS,
   794	      maxOutputBytes: DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES,
   795	      rootPath: WORKSPACE_ROOT,
   796	    };
   797	
   798	    const uniquePaths = Array.from(new Set(input.paths.map((path) => path.trim()).filter(Boolean)));
   799	    const results: ReviewSourceFileReadResult[] = [];
   800	    for (const path of uniquePaths) {
   801	      const toolResult = await executeReviewTool(
   802	        sandbox,
   803	        { type: 'tool', tool: 'read_file', args: { path, maxBytes: effectiveMaxFileBytes } },
   804	        policy,
   805	        effectiveMaxFileBytes
   806	      );
   807	      const resultRecord = asRecord(toolResult.result);
   808	      const error = typeof resultRecord.error === 'string' ? resultRecord.error : null;
   809	      const content = typeof resultRecord.content === 'string' ? resultRecord.content : null;
   810	      const bytes = typeof resultRecord.bytes === 'number' && Number.isFinite(resultRecord.bytes)
   811	        ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(resultRecord.bytes))
   812	        : 0;
   813	      const truncated = Boolean(resultRecord.truncated);
   814	      results.push({ path, content, bytes, truncated, error });
   815	    }
   816	
   817	    return results;
   818	  } finally {
   819	    if (typeof sandbox.destroy === 'function') {
   820	      try {
   821	        await sandbox.destroy();
   822	      } catch {
   823	        // Best-effort cleanup only.
   824	      }
   825	    }
   826	  }
   827	}

function readNullableTimestamp(value: unknown, label: string): string | null {

export function parseGetReviewResponse(payload: unknown): GetReviewResponse {
  const root = asRecord(payload);
  const review = asRecord(root.review);
  if (!root.review || Object.keys(review).length === 0) {
    throw new Error('No review payload in response.');
  }

  const summaryRecord = review.summary === undefined ? null : asRecord(review.summary);
  const findings = Array.isArray(review.findings)
    ? review.findings.map((item) => {
        const finding = asRecord(item);
        const locations = Array.isArray(finding.locations)
          ? finding.locations.map((locationItem) => {
              const location = asRecord(locationItem);
              const startLine = location.startLine;
              const endLine = location.endLine;
              const hasNullRange = startLine === null && endLine === null;
              const hasNumberRange =
                Number.isInteger(startLine) &&
                (startLine as number) > 0 &&
                Number.isInteger(endLine) &&
                (endLine as number) >= (startLine as number);
              if (!hasNullRange && !hasNumberRange) {
                throw new Error('Invalid review payload: finding location line range is invalid.');
              }
              return {
                filePath: readString(location.filePath, 'finding location filePath'),
                startLine: hasNullRange ? null : (startLine as number),
                endLine: hasNullRange ? null : (endLine as number),
              };
            })
          : [];

        return {
          severity: readSeverity(finding.severity),
          category: readCategory(finding.category),
          passType: readPassType(finding.passType),
          description: readString(finding.description, 'finding description'),
          locations,
          suggestedFix: typeof finding.suggestedFix === 'string' ? finding.suggestedFix : '',
        };
      })
    : [];

  const targetRecord = asRecord(review.target);
  const provenanceRecord = asRecord(review.provenance);
  const contextResolutionRecord = asRecord(provenanceRecord.contextResolution);
  const coChangeRecord = asRecord(provenanceRecord.coChange);
  const validationRecord = asRecord(provenanceRecord.validation);
  const furtherPassesSignalRecord = asRecord(provenanceRecord.furtherPassesLowYield);
  const reviewContextRefRecord = asRecord(provenanceRecord.reviewContextRef);
  const reviewContextStatsRecord = asRecord(provenanceRecord.reviewContextStats);
  const reviewedFilesRecord = asRecord(provenanceRecord.reviewedFiles);
  const intentRecord = asRecord(review.intent);
  const errorRecord = asRecord(review.error);

  return {
    review: {
      id: readString(review.id, 'review id'),
      workspaceId: readString(review.workspaceId, 'workspaceId'),
      deploymentId: readString(review.deploymentId, 'deploymentId'),
      target: {

        workspaceId: readString(targetRecord.workspaceId, 'target.workspaceId'),

      },
      mode: readMode(review.mode),
      status: readStatus(review.status),

    40	export async function processReviewRun(env: Env, reviewId: string, options?: ReviewRunExecutionOptions): Promise<void> {
    41	  const claimed = await claimReviewRunForExecution(env.DB, reviewId);
    42	  if (!claimed) {
    43	    const existing = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
    44	    if (existing?.status === 'running') {
    45	      await handleUnclaimedReviewRun(env, reviewId, existing, options?.allowRetryScheduling ?? true);
    46	    }
    47	    return;
    48	  }
    49	
    50	  let review: ReviewRunResponse | null = null;
    51	  try {
    52	    review = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
    53	    if (!review) {
    54	      return;
    55	    }
    56	
    57	    const payload = await getReviewRunRequestPayload(env.DB, reviewId);
    58	    if (!payload) {
    59	      await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'failed', {
    60	        errorCode: 'review_not_found',
    61	        errorMessage: 'Review request payload no longer exists',
    62	      });
    63	      await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
    64	        reviewId,
    65	        eventType: 'review_failed',
    66	        payload: {
    67	          code: 'review_not_found',
    68	          message: 'Review request payload no longer exists',
    69	        },
    70	      });
    71	      return;
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    const reviewContext = await assembleReviewContextBootstrap(env, review, payload, options);
    75	    const report = await executeReviewRun(env, review, payload, reviewContext, options);
    76	    await finalizeSuccessfulReview(env, reviewId, payload, report);
    77	  } catch (error) {
    78	    const message = formatReviewAnalysisError(error, {
    79	      openrouterApiKey=[REDACTED](options?.openrouterApiKey),
    80	    });
    81	    const latest = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
    82	    const attemptCount = latest?.attemptCount ?? review?.attemptCount ?? 0;
    83	
    84	    const allowRetryScheduling = options?.allowRetryScheduling !== false;
    85	    if (allowRetryScheduling && shouldRetryReviewError(error) && attemptCount <= 2) {
    86	      await scheduleReviewRetry(env, reviewId, {
    87	        attemptCount,
    88	        message,
    89	        reason: message.slice(0, 500),
    90	        throwMessage: 'Review transient failure; retry requested',
    91	      });
    92	    }
    93	
    94	    const contextAssemblyErrorCode = error instanceof ReviewContextAssemblyError ? error.code : null;
    95	    const finalErrorCode = contextAssemblyErrorCode ?? 'review_execution_failed';
    96	    await finalizeFailedReview(env, reviewId, {
    97	      errorCode: finalErrorCode,
    98	      message,
    99	      contextAssemblyErrorCode,
   100	    });
   101	  }
   102	}

   104	/**
   105	 * Repeats inline review execution while the persisted state requests another retry cycle.
   106	 */
   107	export async function runReviewInlineWithRetries(
   108	  env: Env,
   109	  reviewId: string,
   110	  maxCycles = 4,
   111	  options?: ReviewRunExecutionOptions
   112	): Promise<void> {
   113	  for (let cycle = 0; cycle < maxCycles; cycle += 1) {
   114	    try {
   115	      await processReviewRun(env, reviewId, options);
   116	    } catch {
   117	      // Retry scheduling is inferred from persisted status.
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    const latest = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
   121	    if (!latest) {
   122	      return;
   123	    }
   124	    if (latest.status !== 'queued') {
   125	      return;
   126	    }
   127	    if (latest.error?.code !== 'retry_scheduled') {
   128	      return;
   129	    }
   130	  }
   131	
   132	  await finalizeInlineRetryExhaustion(env, reviewId);
   133	}

import type { Env, ReviewRunResponse } from '../../types.js';
import {
  appendReviewEvent,
  getReviewRun,
  replaceReviewFindings,
  updateReviewRunStatus,
} from '../db.js';

export class QueueRetryError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'QueueRetryError';
  }
}

const REVIEW_MAX_RETRIES = 2;
const DEFAULT_REVIEW_ATTEMPT_TIMEOUT_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
const REVIEW_STALE_GRACE_MS = 60 * 1000;

function parseTimeoutMs(value: string | undefined, fallback: number): number {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') {
    return fallback;
  }
  const parsed = Number.parseInt(value, 10);
  if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed <= 0) {
    return fallback;
  }
  return parsed;
}

function toTimestampMs(value: string | null): number | null {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.trim()) {
    return null;
  }
  const parsed = Date.parse(value);
  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
}

export function transientReviewFailure(message: string): boolean {
  return /(d1|database is locked|sqlite_busy|temporarily unavailable|connection reset|timed out|timeout|aborted|fetch failed|network)/i.test(message);
}

/**
 * Moves a review back to queued state with a retry marker and clears any partial persisted output.
 */
export async function scheduleReviewRetry(
  env: Env,
  reviewId: string,
  input: {
    attemptCount: number;
    message: string;
    reason: string;
    extraPayload?: Record<string, unknown>;
    throwMessage?: string;
  }
): Promise<never> {
  await replaceReviewFindings(env.DB, reviewId, []);
  await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'queued', {
    report: null,
    markdownSummary: null,
    startedAt: null,
    finishedAt: null,
    errorCode: 'retry_scheduled',
    errorMessage: input.message,
  });
  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
    reviewId,
    eventType: 'review_retry_scheduled',
    payload: {
      attemptCount: input.attemptCount,
      maxRetries: REVIEW_MAX_RETRIES,
      reason: input.reason,
      ...(input.extraPayload ?? {}),
    },
  });
  throw new QueueRetryError(input.throwMessage ?? 'Review retry requested');
}

/**
 * Handles the case where a review could not be claimed because another worker may already be running it.
 * Detects stale-running reviews and either schedules a retry or marks them failed.
 */
export async function handleUnclaimedReviewRun(
  env: Env,
  reviewId: string,
  existing: ReviewRunResponse,
  allowRetryScheduling: boolean
): Promise<void> {
  if (existing.status !== 'running') {
    return;
  }

  const attemptTimeoutMs = parseTimeoutMs(env.ATTEMPT_TIMEOUT_MS, DEFAULT_REVIEW_ATTEMPT_TIMEOUT_MS);
  const staleThresholdMs = attemptTimeoutMs + REVIEW_STALE_GRACE_MS;
  const startedAtMs = toTimestampMs(existing.startedAt) ?? toTimestampMs(existing.updatedAt) ?? toTimestampMs(existing.createdAt);
  const staleForMs = startedAtMs === null ? null : Date.now() - startedAtMs;
  const isStale = typeof staleForMs === 'number' && staleForMs >= staleThresholdMs;
  if (!isStale) {
    throw new QueueRetryError('Review run is already running; defer redelivery');
  }

  const staleForSeconds = Math.floor((staleForMs ?? 0) / 1000);
  const message = `Review execution stalled in running state for ${staleForSeconds}s (timeout threshold ${Math.floor(staleThresholdMs / 1000)}s).`;
  const attemptCount = existing.attemptCount ?? 0;
  if (allowRetryScheduling && attemptCount <= REVIEW_MAX_RETRIES) {
    await scheduleReviewRetry(env, reviewId, {
      attemptCount,
      message,
      reason: 'stale_running_timeout',
      extraPayload: { staleForSeconds },
      throwMessage: 'Review stale-running recovery requested',
    });
  }

  await replaceReviewFindings(env.DB, reviewId, []);
  await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'failed', {
    report: null,
    markdownSummary: null,
    errorCode: 'review_execution_timeout',
    errorMessage: message,
  });
  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
    reviewId,
    eventType: 'review_failed',
    payload: {
      code: 'review_execution_timeout',
      message,
    },
  });
}

/**
 * Final fallback when repeated inline retries leave the review stuck in queued/retry-scheduled state.
 */
export async function finalizeInlineRetryExhaustion(env: Env, reviewId: string): Promise<void> {
  const latest = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
  if (latest?.status === 'queued' && latest.error?.code === 'retry_scheduled') {
    const message = `Review ${reviewId} remained queued after inline retries`;
    await replaceReviewFindings(env.DB, reviewId, []);
    await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'failed', {
      report: null,
      markdownSummary: null,
      errorCode: 'review_execution_failed',
      errorMessage: message,
    });
    await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
      reviewId,
      eventType: 'review_failed',
      payload: {
        code: 'review_execution_failed',
        message,
      },
    });
  }
}

export function shouldRetryReviewError(error: unknown): boolean {
  if (error instanceof QueueRetryError) {
    return true;
  }
  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  return transientReviewFailure(message);
}
